- **zhengzong-mingli**: 正统命理大师（子平八字 + 奇门遁甲）。严格以《滴天髓》《子平真诠》《穷通宝鉴》《三命通会》《渊海子平》 为主要依据，凡古籍无据者不妄断；禁止套话与迎合，只输出最有命理依据、应象最强、可验证性最高的结论； 结论先行、不模棱两可、不说圆滑话，然后再给解释。输出前强制走"出结果审核关卡"（查编造、查修正、 查串盘、查循环论证、查排盘校验）。触发词：算八字、看八字、批八字、排盘、命理、算命、四柱、大运、流年、 奇门遁甲、奇门、起局、看运势、命运分析、bazi、qimen、滴天髓、子平真诠、穷通宝鉴、三命通会、渊海子平。 ([ambin-d/ambin-skills](https://github.com/ambin-d/ambin-skills/tree/HEAD/zhengzong-mingli))

- **zhi-bi-zhi-ji**: 何时调用：当用户在竞争中缺乏对对手/环境的充分了解，或需要建立信息优势时。 关键信号："对手什么情况？"、"我不了解这个市场"、"信息不对称怎么办？"。 何时不调用：用户已有充分信息需要决策（用 xian-sheng-hou-zhan），或用户需要的是欺骗对手（用 bing-zhe-gui-dao）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/sun-zi-bing-fa/zhi-bi-zhi-ji))
- **zhi-du-xing-shuai**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解组织兴衰的规律时。 关键信号："组织怎么兴衰？"、"创业到衰落怎么走？"、"腐化从哪开始？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是居安思危（用 ju-an-si-wei），或用户需要的是历史周期律（用 li-shi-zhou-qi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/zi-zhi-tong-jian/zhi-du-xing-shuai))
- **zhi-hui-zhi-lu**: 当用户需要"否定法(Neti neti)""如何趋近不可言说之物""以减法去蔽"的方法,或读到《广林奥义书》"非此非此"时调用。适用于概念焦虑、执著名相、冥想入门;不适用于需明确肯定的科学定义。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/ao-yi-shu/zhi-hui-zhi-lu))
- **zhi-ji-zhi-bi**: 何时调用：当用户需要了解自己才能了解别人，或需要进行自我修炼时。 关键信号："怎么了解自己？"、"自知才能知人吗？"、"怎么自我修炼？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是抵巇术（用 di-xi-shu），或用户需要的是捭阖之道（用 bai-he-zhi-dao）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/gui-gu-zi/zhi-ji-zhi-bi))
- **zhi-liang-zhi**: 何时调用：当用户面临道德困境需要内在判断，或在复杂情境中需要回归本心做决策时。 关键信号："这件事该不该做？""我心里觉得不对但又说不清为什么""怎么做才是对的？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是逻辑分析（用 mao-xuan 的 maodun-fenxi），或用户需要的是规则遵循（用 han-fei-zi 的 yi-fa-zhi-guo）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/yang-ming-xin-xue/zhi-liang-zhi))

- **zhi-min-di**: 当用户分析"殖民地体系如何体现重商逻辑及其后果""美洲发现对欧洲商业的意义"时调用。 关键信号：殖民地/重商体系/航海条例/美洲发现。不适用于为殖民扩张辩护。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/guo-fu-lun/zhi-min-di))
- **zhi-ren-shan-ren**: 何时调用：当用户需要识别人才、用人所长，或需要理解领导者的核心能力时。 关键信号："怎么识别人才？"、"怎么用好人？"、"领导者的核心能力是什么？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是性格分析（用 xing-ge-ji-yun），或用户需要的是功高震主（用 gong-gao-zhen-zhu）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/shi-ji/zhi-ren-shan-ren))

- **zhougong-dream-interpretation**: 周公解梦 + 阴阳五行 + 天人感应 + 现代心理学 的多层梦境解析技能。当用户描述梦境（做梦、梦见、梦到、解梦、周公解梦、圆梦、周公、dream interpretation）时使用：先按类别检索《周公解梦》原文吉凶断语，再以《周易》阴阳八卦、《尚书·洪范》《白虎通义》《春秋繁露》五行学说与天人感应推演（结合本命纳音五行、做梦当天当地天气；用户若提供近期特殊事件，则做梦事合参/同类相动推演），最后辅以弗洛伊德/荣格/睡眠科学等心理学视角，并给出联合三层的交叉解析，形成「文化参照 + 心理洞察」式解读（非字面预言）。内置公历转农历、属相、纳音五行脚本与经纬度定位、历史天气查询脚本。即使用户只是随口提到做了个梦、梦见某物、想知道梦的含义，也应主动使用本技能。 ([tf1993614/Know-your-fate](https://github.com/tf1993614/Know-your-fate/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/zhougong-dream-interpretation) · ★14)

- **zimmer-change-ai-artifact**: Add or change an AI artifact in this repo — a skill, agent root, MCP server, plugin, hook, or reference. Zimmer ships a SELF-CONTAINED AIR catalog: the top-level artifact indexes wired by `air.json` are the catalog, resolved offline with no network and no private GitHub catalogs. Covers where each artifact type lives, how `default_in_roots` makes an artifact default-on, the resolution invariants the whole test suite depends on, and how to verify a change before pushing. An artifact change is NOT an app bug — do not go looking for one in `app/`. ([tadasant/zimmer](https://github.com/tadasant/zimmer/tree/HEAD/skills/zimmer-change-ai-artifact) · ★1)
- **zimmer-debug-staging**: Diagnose a broken or silent Zimmer staging deployment. Covers the four places staging's truth lives (the deploy run, the containers on the droplet, the OTLP logs in VictoriaLogs, the errors in GlitchTip), the `bin/rails obs:status` / `obs:smoke` diagnostics that exist because Zimmer's telemetry is a HARD NO-OP when misconfigured, LogsQL queries scoped to `deployment.environment=staging`, and the specific failure modes that make staging look healthy while doing nothing. Use when a staging deploy fails, when sessions hang, or when telemetry is missing. ([tadasant/zimmer](https://github.com/tadasant/zimmer/tree/HEAD/skills/zimmer-debug-staging) · ★1)
- **zimmer-deploy-staging**: Deploy a Zimmer change to the staging environment. Staging is a PERSISTENT Kamal-deployed droplet, and the `Deploy staging` workflow is manual-dispatch only — it takes a `ref` input, so it can build and deploy an UNMERGED feature branch, which is the main reason to use it. Covers dispatching the run, the Kamal container swap, watching the deploy, rolling back, and what an agent session can and cannot do without DO/Tailscale credentials. There is NO production deploy workflow in this repo. ([tadasant/zimmer](https://github.com/tadasant/zimmer/tree/HEAD/skills/zimmer-deploy-staging) · ★1)
- **zimmer-run-tests**: Run the right slice of Zimmer's test suite for a change and reproduce CI locally. Covers the four tiers (Minitest unit/integration, Capybara system tests, Playwright e2e scripts, the standalone GHCR retention test), which of them CI actually gates on, the Postgres + Redis prerequisites, and the lint / Brakeman / Gemfile.lock jobs that fail PRs as often as the tests do. Use before pushing any change to this repo. ([tadasant/zimmer](https://github.com/tadasant/zimmer/tree/HEAD/skills/zimmer-run-tests) · ★1)
- **zimmer-start-dev-server**: Bring up a local Zimmer instance (Rails 8 + Tailwind watcher via foreman) to develop against, exercise a change by hand, or take screenshots. Covers the Postgres + Redis prerequisites the app does NOT provision for you, the `bin/dev` vs `bin/setup` split, port handling, and the failure modes that look like a broken app but are really a missing service. Use before any change that needs to be verified in a running app rather than only in tests. ([tadasant/zimmer](https://github.com/tadasant/zimmer/tree/HEAD/skills/zimmer-start-dev-server) · ★1)

- **ziniao-staff**: 紫鸟员工管理：员工的查询、创建、修改、启用/禁用/删除和调岗。当用户需要查询员工信息、创建新员工、修改员工信息、启用/禁用/删除员工或调整员工部门时使用。 ([ziniao-open/skills](https://github.com/ziniao-open/skills) · 4.9k installs)

- **zins-assess-network-security**: Assess network security posture using Zscaler Analytics (Z-Insights). - **zins-audit-shadow-it**: Audit shadow IT and SaaS application usage using Zscaler Analytics (Z-Insights). Use when a security team asks: 'What shadow IT apps are being used?', 'Show me unsanctioned SaaS usage', 'What is our SaaS risk exposure?', 'How many IoT devices are on our network?', or 'Generate a shadow IT report.' ([zscaler/zscaler-mcp-server](https://github.com/zscaler/zscaler-mcp-server/tree/HEAD/skills/zins/audit-shadow-it) · ★51)
- **zins-investigate-security-incident**: Investigate security incidents using Zscaler Z-Insights analytics. - **zion-prd-ajuda**: Ajuda de bolso do harness Zion Build PRD — tira dúvidas sobre os estágios, os comandos `/zion-*`, os artefatos que cada um produz e a costura com o Spec Kit, ancorando cada afirmação na fonte. Use quando o usuário mencionar explicitamente o harness Zion Build PRD, um estágio dele (descoberta, estudo, spike/ADR, PRD, decomposição, pontes, trace, dia 2) ou um comando `zion-*` e perguntar "como funciona", "qual comando resolve", "onde isso entra", "o que vem depois" ou "o que o Spec Kit faz aqui". Não lê nem grava artefato do projeto: tarefa disfarçada é roteada ao comando dono. ([tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd](https://github.com/tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd/tree/HEAD/skills/zion-prd-ajuda))
- **zion-prd-constitution-prompt**: Ponte do harness Zion Build PRD para o Spec Kit (bootstrap, uma vez por projeto) — monta o prompt do /speckit.constitution derivando princípios decidíveis e rastreáveis dos NFRs/restrições da PRD, entrega pronto e para. Use quando for iniciar a constitution do Spec Kit a partir de uma PRD; não dispara o /speckit.* por você. ([tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd](https://github.com/tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd/tree/HEAD/skills/zion-prd-constitution-prompt))
- **zion-prd-decompose**: Estágio 4 do harness Zion Build PRD — transforma os RF-xx da PRD em épicos, story map e specs verticais validadas por INVEST, e injeta a tabela de rastreabilidade. Use para "decompor a PRD", "fatiar em histórias/épicos" ou "montar o backlog vertical" depois que a PRD estiver escrita. ([tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd](https://github.com/tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd/tree/HEAD/skills/zion-prd-decompose))
- **zion-prd-discovery**: Estágio 1 do harness Zion Build PRD — conduz a descoberta enxuta de produto (visão em 1 frase, persona nomeada, quadro faz/não-faz) e grava docs/discovery.md; idempotente — retoma/revisa um discovery existente sem sobrescrever. Use ao iniciar um produto/feature a partir de uma ideia bruta, antes de qualquer PRD ou stack, ou para retomar/revisar a descoberta em nova sessão, sempre que o usuário quiser "começar a descoberta", "destrinchar a ideia", "definir visão e escopo" ou "continuar/revisar o discovery". ([tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd](https://github.com/tuyoshivinicius/zion-build-prd/tree/HEAD/skills/zion-prd-discovery))
- **zion-prd-estudo**: Estágio 0 (opcional) do harness Zion Build PRD — estudo pré-discovery de UM candidato: edge cases, 2–4 alternativas comparadas (sempre incluindo "não fazer"), ROI justificado e recomendação não vinculante, gravado em docs/estudos/&lt;slug&gt;.md do projeto-alvo.
